[EVENT: PSE35340]
Date: 2023-08-04
Country: Palestine | Location: Nur ash Shams Camp
Type: Battles > Armed clash | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: PIJ: Katibat Tulkarm [Political militia]
Actor2: Military Forces of Israel (2022-) [External/Other forces]
Fatalities: 1
Sources: Al-Ittihad; Al Ghad (Jordan); AP; Arab 48; Arutz Sheva; Dunia Al Watan; Haaretz; Jerusalem Post; Ma'an News Agency; Palestine News and Information Agency; Palestinian News Network; PLO Negotiations Affairs Department; Times of Israel [Other-National]

[EVENT: PSE35368]

Also, 2 Palestinian rioters were injured by stone-throwing.

[EVENT: PSE35669]
Date: 2023-08-18
Country: Palestine | Location: Al Quds - Shuafat Camp
Type: Battles > Armed clash | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Police Forces of Israel (2022-) Border Police [External/Other forces]
Actor2: Unidentified Armed Group (Palestine) [Political militia]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Haaretz; Newpress; Palestine News and Information Agency; Times of Israel; Twitter [New media-National]

DESCRIPTION:
On 18 August 2023, Israeli border police shot fire at a Palestinian car in Al Quds - Shuafat Camp (Al Quds, West Bank), injuring the driver. Police said the driver accelerated the car toward police officers before being shot [presumably in an intentional car ramming]. During the arrests, 2 other passengers threw stones at the officers, who responded with riot dispersal measures and arrested the 2 as well. None of the Israeli officers were injured.

[EVENT: PSE35491]
Date: 2023-08-18
Country: Palestine | Location: Al Quds - Shuafat Camp
Type: Violence against civilians > Attack | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Police Forces of Israel (2022-) Border Police [External/Other forces]
Actor2: Civilians (Palestine) [Civilians]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Haaretz; Palestine News and Information Agency [National-Regional]

DESCRIPTION:
On 18 August 2023, Israeli police and police border forces severely beat a Palestinian suspected of drug possession during an arrest operation in Al Quds - Mukhayyam Shuafat refugee camp (Al Quds, West Bank). He was injured and the Star of David was branded on his face. The Police said that they used force during the arrest and the mark on his face was caused by an officer's shoes. Forensic analysis concluded that the sign on the face was caused by the use of a metallic object.
